# Shortcut ohosTest 用例说明

[中文说明](<README_zh.md>) · [English](<README.md>)

## 验证状态

**全部待验证：这批修改未执行编译、构建、类型检查、应用或设备测试。** 下表来自现有源码中的 @tc.number/@tc.name/@tc.desc 和调用/断言摘录，不是执行报告，不包含虚构PASS或截图。

## 环境与执行约束

- 匹配SDK26的OpenHarmony标准系统、DevEco工具链和本地签名；测试bundle为com.example.shortcut，主模块entry，隔离feature模块entry_test。
- 由 [List.test.ets](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/List.test.ets>) 注册，Runner保留延迟导入；先注册delegator再加载原生UiTest导出。
- 现有runCase保留10秒watchdog和逆序cleanup；原生Preferences用例使用sc_recycle_native_qa_前缀的独立命名空间并在finally删除；不得清空生产workspace/journal。
- 原生UI只操作FeatureQaAbility的白名单RecycleUiQa页面，按稳定组件ID定位，清理仅终止自己持有的Ability。纯规则/受控存储用例不代表桌面实际支持情况。
- 建议按纯规则→受控存储/显隐/路由→原生持久化→隔离UI顺序验证，再执行全量入口，收集begin/failure/end日志。执行者记录设备/OS/SDK/工具链、命令、日期和结果；所有待验证状态须以真实结果更新。
- 人工补充：英文/中文语言切换后重新进入页面，验证长文案和占位参数；长按桌面入口验证冷/热启动；外部修改显隐后检查保留/覆盖确认；失败、未知写结果及跨进程恢复不重复写入；深色模式和无障碍布局。

## 用例清单

每条步骤：1. 按对应源码建立隔离夹具/输入；2. 执行“动作摘录”所列真实调用（完整顺序见源码）；3. 核对该用例全部expect断言并释放夹具。“断言摘录”只用于定位，不能替代完整用例。

### [AppNavigation.test.ets](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/AppNavigation.test.ets>) — 8条

| 用例编号 / 名称 | 场景、步骤与动作摘录 | 预期 / 断言摘录 | 状态 |
|---|---|---|---|
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_PRODUCTIONAPPNAVIGATION_0100](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/AppNavigation.test.ets#L68>)<br>testProductionAppNavigation0001 | Verify cold want waits for store and applies today open<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. nav.receiveWant → nav.activate → store.initialize → readGate.resolve；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify cold want waits for store and applies today open<br><code>expect(parseLaunchRequest(want('todo.list', 'todayOpen'), true).filter).assertEqual('todayOpen'); expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(0); expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(1);</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_PRODUCTIONAPPNAVIGATION_0200](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/AppNavigation.test.ets#L99>)<br>testProductionAppNavigation0002 | Verify duplicate pending and same page want uses production store<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. nav.activate → nav.receiveWant → gate.resolve → store.setFavoritesOnly → store.setCollectionQuery；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify duplicate pending and same page want uses production store<br><code>expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(1); expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(1); expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(2);</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_PRODUCTIONAPPNAVIGATION_0300](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/AppNavigation.test.ets#L140>)<br>testProductionAppNavigation0003 | Verify leave cancel and save failure never navigate or apply context<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. store.setDraftTitle → store.saveNote → store.beginEditNote → nav.activate → nav.receiveWant → store.hasUnsavedNoteWork；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify leave cancel and save failure never navigate or apply context<br><code>expect(await store.saveNote()).assertTrue(); expect(dialogs.calls).assertEqual(1); expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(0);</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_PRODUCTIONAPPNAVIGATION_0400](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/AppNavigation.test.ets#L181>)<br>testProductionAppNavigation0004 | Verify route failure rolls back filter and successful leave save remains saved<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. store.setDraftTitle → store.saveNote → store.beginEditNote → scope.cleanup → nav.dispose → nav.initialize → nav.activate；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify route failure rolls back filter and successful leave save remains saved<br><code>expect(await store.saveNote()).assertTrue(); expect(store.snapshot().favoritesOnly).assertFalse(); expect(store.snapshot().notes[0].title).assertEqual('已保存修改');</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_PRODUCTIONAPPNAVIGATION_0500](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/AppNavigation.test.ets#L218>)<br>testProductionAppNavigation0005 | Verify late dialog and canceled pending do not run after dispose<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. store.setDraftTitle → nav.activate → nav.receiveWant → gate.resolve → nav.dispose → store.hasUnsavedNoteWork；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify late dialog and canceled pending do not run after dispose<br><code>expect(dialogs.calls).assertEqual(1); expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(0); expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(0);</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_PRODUCTIONAPPNAVIGATION_0600](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/AppNavigation.test.ets#L253>)<br>testProductionAppNavigation0006 | Verify pending detail retains id when cold or busy<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. nav.activate → nav.openDetail → store.initialize → store.setDraftTitle → store.keepDraft → writeGate.resolve；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify pending detail retains id when cold or busy<br><code>expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(0); expect(routes.calls[0]).assertEqual('push:pages/ShortcutDetailPage:sc.note.new'); expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(1);</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_PRODUCTIONAPPNAVIGATION_0700](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/AppNavigation.test.ets#L288>)<br>testProductionAppNavigation0007 | Verify old dialog cannot clear reinitialized session pending<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. store.initialize → store.setDraftTitle → scope.cleanup → nav.dispose → nav.initialize → nav.activate → nav.receiveWant；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify old dialog cannot clear reinitialized session pending<br><code>expect(reports).assertEqual(0); expect(dialogs.toasts).assertEqual(0); expect(await saving).assertTrue();</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_PRODUCTIONAPPNAVIGATION_0800](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/AppNavigation.test.ets#L333>)<br>testProductionAppNavigation0008 | A home activation before back resolves keeps its handler and permits the next real route.<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. nav.activate → nav.back → backGate.resolve → nav.open；3. 核对断言并清理。 | A home activation before back resolves keeps its handler and permits the next real route.<br><code>expect(routes.calls[0]).assertEqual('back:false'); expect(homeRevisits).assertEqual(1); expect(routes.calls.length).assertEqual(2);</code> | 待验证 |

| [SUB_SHORTCUT_BUSINESSCOVERAGE_1700](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/BusinessCoverage.test.ets#L541>)<br>testBusinessCoverage0017 | Verify date refresh and reset write failure preserve committed state<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. store.setTodoDone → clock.setDate → store.refreshTodos → store.resetToday → todos.every；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify date refresh and reset write failure preserve committed state<br><code>expect(await store.refreshTodos()).assertFalse(); expect(store.snapshot().todoDate).assertEqual('2026-11-02'); expect(store.snapshot().todos[0].done).assertTrue();</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_BUSINESSCOVERAGE_1800](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/BusinessCoverage.test.ets#L572>)<br>testBusinessCoverage0018 | Verify local date key handles leap day month year and single digits<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. 执行源码中的输入/动作；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify local date key handles leap day month year and single digits<br><code>expect(localDateKey(new Date(2024, 1, 29, 0, 0, 0).getTime())).assertEqual('2024-02-29'); expect(localDateKey(new Date(2026, 0, 2, 23, 59, 59).getTime())).assertEqual('2026-01-02'); expect(localDateKey(new Date(2026, 11, 31, 23, 59, 59).getTime())).assertEqual('2026-12-31');</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_BUSINESSCOVERAGE_1900](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/BusinessCoverage.test.ets#L589>)<br>testBusinessCoverage0019 | Verify reopened same timestamp never creates duplicate note ids<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. first.setDraftTitle → first.saveNote → first.dispose → reopened.setDraftTitle → reopened.saveNote → reopened.dispose；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify reopened same timestamp never creates duplicate note ids<br><code>expect(await first.saveNote()).assertTrue(); expect(await reopened.saveNote()).assertTrue(); expect(notes.length).assertEqual(2);</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_BUSINESSCOVERAGE_2000](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/BusinessCoverage.test.ets#L620>)<br>testBusinessCoverage0020 | Verify restored ids with sequence gaps are never overwritten<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. clock.now → JSON.stringify → store.setDraftTitle → store.saveNote → notes.map；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify restored ids with sequence gaps are never overwritten<br><code>expect(await store.saveNote()).assertTrue(); expect(await store.saveNote()).assertTrue(); expect(notes.length).assertEqual(4);</code> | 待验证 |

| [SUB_SHORTCUT_TODODATEHISTORY_0100](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/TodoDateCases.test.ets#L32>)<br>testTodoDateHistory0001 | Verify past date writes stay on selected day<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. store.addTodo → clock.setDate → store.refreshTodos → store.selectTodoDate → todos.some；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify past date writes stay on selected day<br><code>expect(await store.addTodo('今日任务')).assertTrue(); expect(await store.refreshTodos()).assertTrue(); expect(store.snapshot().todoDate).assertEqual('2026-11-03');</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_TODODATEHISTORY_0200](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/TodoDateCases.test.ets#L70>)<br>testTodoDateHistory0002 | Verify invalid and future dates are rejected<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. store.selectTodoDate → store.errorText；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify invalid and future dates are rejected<br><code>expect(validDateKey('2026-11-02')).assertTrue(); expect(validDateKey('2026-02-30')).assertFalse(); expect(validDateKey('2026-13-01')).assertFalse();</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_TODODATEHISTORY_0300](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/TodoDateCases.test.ets#L98>)<br>testTodoDateHistory0003 | Verify date shift stops at today<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. store.shiftTodoDate；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify date shift stops at today<br><code>expect(store.shiftTodoDate(-1)).assertTrue(); expect(store.snapshot().todoDate).assertEqual('2026-11-01'); expect(store.shiftTodoDate(0)).assertTrue();</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_TODODATEHISTORY_0400](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/TodoDateCases.test.ets#L125>)<br>testTodoDateHistory0004 | Verify historical selection survives midnight<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. store.selectTodoDate → store.addTodo → todos.find → clock.setDate → store.setTodoDone → availableDates.indexOf；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify historical selection survives midnight<br><code>expect(store.selectTodoDate('2026-11-01')).assertTrue(); expect(await store.addTodo('昨天任务')).assertTrue(); expect(await store.setTodoDone(first.id, true)).assertTrue();</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_TODODATEHISTORY_0500](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/TodoDateCases.test.ets#L156>)<br>testTodoDateHistory0005 | Verify summaries are descending and counted<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. clock.setDate → store.refreshTodos；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify summaries are descending and counted<br><code>expect(await store.refreshTodos()).assertTrue(); expect(await store.refreshTodos()).assertTrue(); expect(dates.length >= 3).assertTrue();</code> | 待验证 |
| [SUB_SHORTCUT_TODODATEHISTORY_0600](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/TodoDateCases.test.ets#L186>)<br>testTodoDateHistory0006 | Verify historical write persists across reopen<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. store.selectTodoDate → store.addTodo → store.dispose → reopened.selectTodoDate → todos.some；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify historical write persists across reopen<br><code>expect(store.selectTodoDate('2026-10-31')).assertTrue(); expect(await store.addTodo('月末补记')).assertTrue(); expect(reopened.selectTodoDate('2026-10-31')).assertTrue();</code> | 待验证 |

| [SUB_SHORTCUT_VISIBILITYBATCHPRODUCTIONSERVICE_0800](<entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/VisibilityBatch.test.ets#L290>)<br>testVisibilityBatchProductionService0008 | Verify external change requires choice and post confirm change invalidates preview<br>1. 初始化源码夹具；2. service.previewBatchRestore → service.executeBatchPreview → service.previewBatch → batchMessage.includes；3. 核对断言并清理。 | Verify external change requires choice and post confirm change invalidates preview<br><code>expect(preview(service).items[0].externalChange).assertTrue(); expect(api.calls.length).assertEqual(1); expect(api.calls.length).assertEqual(2);</code> | 待验证 |
